Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in tokenization theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 189 and the invariant I(A,B) = 221 mod 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to tokenization theory, we decompose the problem into 189 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 221. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
